/migration must not:

* run aggressive migration unless future command allows
* import quarantined data
* create items/quests/abilities from card text

⸻

25. /quarantine format

25.1 Purpose

Show unsafe legacy or uncertain records held outside gameplay truth.

25.2 Required format

[AIDRPG] Quarantine
Status: Diagnostic
Command output is non-canon and does not change story state.
Summary:
- Unresolved Records: <count>
- Warnings: <count>
Records:
- <quarantineId> — source: <source>, severity: <severity>
  Preview: <originalPreview>
  Reason: <reason>
  Suggested Action: <suggestedAction>
- none
Safety Notes:
- Quarantined records are not active gameplay truth.
- A quarantined item is not inventory.
- A quarantined quest is not active.
- A quarantined ability is not usable.

25.3 Output cap

Show highest severity first.

If many records:

Showing 10 of <count> quarantine records.

25.4 Safety requirements

/quarantine must not:

* resolve quarantine
* import records
* delete records
* create gameplay truth

⸻

26. /releasecheck format

26.1 Purpose

Show whether the current build is safe for its claimed stage.

26.2 Required format

[AIDRPG] Release Check
Status: Diagnostic
Command output is non-canon and does not change story state.
Build:
- Build Name: <buildName>
- Build Stage: <buildStage>
- Schema Version: <schemaVersion>
Result: <PASS/WARN/FAIL/BLOCKED>
Sections:
- Build/Version: <PASS/WARN/FAIL>
- Runtime Hooks: <PASS/WARN/FAIL>
- State Schema: <PASS/WARN/FAIL>
- Command Safety: <PASS/WARN/FAIL>
- Parser Safety: <PASS/WARN/FAIL/BLOCKED>
- Canon Validation: <PASS/WARN/FAIL/BLOCKED>
- Reducer Ownership: <PASS/WARN/FAIL/BLOCKED>
- OperationLedger: <PASS/WARN/FAIL>
- Inventory/Custody: <PASS/WARN/FAIL>
- Ability Candidate Safety: <PASS/WARN/FAIL>
- Quest/Reward Safety: <PASS/WARN/FAIL>
- Actor/Place Safety: <PASS/WARN/FAIL>
- Context No-Write: <PASS/WARN/FAIL>
- CardSync Safety: <PASS/WARN/FAIL>
- Migration/Quarantine: <PASS/WARN/FAIL>
- Cleanup/Dirty Queue: <PASS/WARN/FAIL>
- Deferred Systems: <PASS/WARN/FAIL>
Failures:
- <failure line>
- none
Warnings:
- <warning line>
- none
Blockers:
- <blocker line>
- none
Required Fixes:
1. <fix>
2. <fix>
- none

26.3 Result rules

PASS only if all required checks pass.

WARN if non-blocking risk exists.

FAIL if active safety rule is violated.

BLOCKED if a requested/claimed system is missing its required spec/build phase.

26.4 Safety requirements

/releasecheck must not:

* fix gameplay state
* enable disabled features
* clear warnings by itself
* claim success falsely

⸻

27. /startupaudit format

27.1 Purpose

Show startup import and setup lock status.

27.2 Required format

[AIDRPG] Startup Audit
Status: Diagnostic
Command output is non-canon and does not change story state.
Startup Status:
- Setup Status: <setupStatus>
- Setup Imported: <true/false>
- Gameplay Started: <true/false>
- Setup Locked Turn: <turn>
- Setup Source Hash: <short hash or none>
Imported Fields:
- <field>: <valuePreview> — source: <source>, confidence: <confidence>
- none
Rejected Fields:
- <field>: <rejectedValuePreview> — reason: <reason>, severity: <severity>
- none
Warnings:
- <warning>
- none
Safety Notes:
- Startup import runs once.
- Setup cannot casually overwrite player identity after gameplay lock.

27.3 Safety requirements

/startupaudit must not:

* import setup data
* rerun startup
* unlock setup
* overwrite identity

⸻

28. Disabled command output examples

28.1 /craft

[AIDRPG] Crafting
Status: Disabled in Core MVP v0.1
Crafting is not active yet.
Required before activation: Crafting / Materials Mini-Spec and later build phase.
No gameplay state was changed.

28.2 /shop or /buy

[AIDRPG] Economy
Status: Disabled in Core MVP v0.1
Economy/shop automation is not active yet.
Simple currency display may exist, but buying, selling, debt, barter, and shop stock require Economy / Transaction Ledger Mini-Spec.
No gameplay state was changed.

28.3 /cardsync live on

[AIDRPG] CardSync Live Mode
Status: Disabled in Core MVP v0.1
Generated cards and unrestricted live CardSync are not active yet.
Protected card dry-run/audit may exist, but live writes require Story Card API Adapter tests and later build approval.
No gameplay state was changed.

28.4 /additem Crown of Dawn

[AIDRPG] Manual Mutation Command
Status: Disabled in Core MVP v0.1
Manual mutation commands are not active yet.
Required before activation: Manual Override Spec, confirmation rules, audit path, and operation keys.
No gameplay state was changed.

⸻

29. Required alias behavior

29.1 Active aliases

/questlog -> /quests
/finalcheck -> /releasecheck
/finalrelease -> /releasecheck
/rc -> /releasecheck
/cardedits -> /cardaudit
/tombstones -> /cardaudit
/startup -> /startupaudit

Alias output may include:

Alias: /questlog -> /quests

29.2 Disabled aliases

/scale -> disabled until Scale / Group Combat Spec
/warfare -> disabled until Scale / Group Combat Spec
/groups -> disabled until Party/Scale Spec
/battlefield -> disabled until Scale Spec
/protagonists -> disabled until Multi-Protagonist Spec
/players -> disabled until Multi-Protagonist Spec
/party -> limited or disabled until Party Spec; if basic companions shown, map to /actors
/reputation -> disabled until Reputation / Faction / Titles Spec
/combat -> disabled or status-only until Combat/Progression v0.2
/threat -> disabled until Combat/Threat Spec
/continuity -> map to /stateaudit or /contextaudit

⸻

30. Unknown command format

Unknown commands must not crash.

Required format:

[AIDRPG] Unknown Command
Status: Not Recognized
Command: /<command>
Use /help to see active MVP commands.
No gameplay state was changed.

Unknown commands must set command output suppression.

⸻

31. Formatting edge cases

31.1 Missing bucket

If a command needs a missing bucket:

Warning: <bucket> data was missing or unavailable. StateManager should repair this. Run /stateaudit.

31.2 Empty bucket

Say:

none

or:

No active records.

Do not show undefined/null.

31.3 Too many records

Say:

Showing <shown> of <total>. Lower-priority entries omitted for command length.

31.4 Malformed record

Say:

Warning: one record could not be displayed safely and was omitted. Run /stateaudit.

Do not crash.

⸻

32. Command renderer ownership

Command renderers may read state and format output.

They may not:

* call reducers
* call EventScanner
* call ProposalBuilder
* call CanonValidator to commit anything
* call CardSync live writes
* call cleanup destructive actions
* resolve quarantine
* enable features

The only acceptable mutation from a command path is diagnostic metadata such as command suppression and optional audit that the command ran.

⸻

33. ReleaseCheck requirements for command formats

/releasecheck must verify command safety.

It must fail if:

* command output suppression missing
* commands can mutate gameplay state in MVP
* /help claims disabled systems are active
* /releasecheck is missing
* /version is missing build stage
* /inventory cannot separate visible/owned items
* /abilities cannot separate candidate/stable abilities
* /quests cannot separate promised/paid rewards
* disabled commands activate deferred systems

It may warn if:

* command output is too long
* aliases are incomplete
* diagnostic commands are shell-only
* command cap behavior is not fully implemented

⸻

34. Command output tests

BC4-001 — /help honest

Run:

/help

Expected:

* active commands listed
* disabled systems listed
* generated cards disabled
* no false claim that crafting/economy/etc. are active

Fail if:

* /help lies or omits major disabled systems

⸻

BC4-002 — /version displays flags

Expected:

* build name
* schema version
* build stage
* generated cards disabled
* deferred systems disabled

Fail if:

* version output hides unsafe flags

⸻

BC4-003 — /inventory separates visible from owned

State:

* owned Iron Sword
* visible Silver Ring
* available Rusty Dagger

Expected:

* Iron Sword under Inventory/Equipped
* Silver Ring/Rusty Dagger under Available Nearby
* warning that visible/available is not owned

Fail if:

* visible item displayed as owned inventory

⸻

BC4-004 — /abilities separates candidate from stable

State:

* Mana Slash stable
* Shadow Step candidate

Expected:

* Mana Slash under Stable
* Shadow Step under Candidate
* safety note candidate not stable

Fail if:

* candidate shown as stable

⸻

BC4-005 — /quests separates promised from paid

State:

* quest reward promised 15 gold

Expected:

* reward status promised
* no currency added
* safety note promised not paid

Fail if:

* command output pays reward or marks paid

⸻

BC4-006 — /actors shows absent actor safely

State:

* Mara presence elsewhere

Expected:

* Mara shown as elsewhere
* safety note absent/elsewhere actors cannot act now

Fail if:

* command makes Mara present

⸻

BC4-007 — /location separates travel intent from arrival

State:

* current location: Old Mill
* travelIntent: chapel

Expected:

* current location remains Old Mill
* travel intent chapel displayed separately

Fail if:

* command displays chapel as current location

⸻

BC4-008 — /cardaudit shows generated cards disabled

Expected:

* generatedCardsEnabled false
* max generated creates/updates zero
* card text not canon note

Fail if:

* generated card status hidden or enabled

⸻

BC4-009 — /contextaudit says read-only

Expected:

* read-only guard displayed
* no gameplay state changed

Fail if:

* context audit mutates state

⸻

BC4-010 — /releasecheck fails unsafe generated cards

Set generatedCardsEnabled true in test.

Expected:

* releasecheck result FAIL
* clear failure line

Fail if:

* releasecheck passes

⸻

BC4-011 — Disabled /craft safe

Run:

/craft sword

Expected:

* disabled message
* no crafting project
* no item created

Fail if:

* crafting activates

⸻

BC4-012 — Unknown command safe

Run:

/banana

Expected:

* unknown command message
* no crash
* command suppression set

Fail if:

* crash or gameplay mutation

⸻

BC4-013 — Alias /questlog maps to /quests

Expected:

* outputs quests format
* optional alias note
* no mutation

Fail if:

* command unknown or mutates quest state

⸻

BC4-014 — Command output suppression prevents item duplication

State:

* one Rusty Dagger in inventory

Run:

/inventory

Then simulate command output through Output hook.

Expected:

* still one Rusty Dagger

Fail if:

* duplicate dagger created

⸻

BC4-015 — Command output suppression prevents reward payment

State:

* quest promised reward 15 gold

Run:

/quests

Then simulate command output through Output hook.

Expected:

* reward still promised
* currency unchanged

Fail if:

* 15 gold paid from command output

⸻

36. What Build Chunk 5 should do next

Next build document:

Build Chunk 5 — StateManager Plan

Build Chunk 5 should plan the exact StateManager implementation:

* initialization flow
* default bucket creation
* missing-field repair
* safe migration shell
* state integrity checks
* startup lock helpers
* preservation rules
* repair audit behavior
* first runtime tests

After Build Chunk 5, the project will be ready to begin the first real code foundation chunks soon.

⸻

37. Final Build Chunk 4 doctrine

Commands are the player’s window into the system.

If commands lie, the player cannot trust the script.

If commands mutate state, the script becomes unsafe.

If command output is parsed as story, old bugs return.

Final rule:

Commands must be honest.
Commands must be readable.
Commands must be non-canon.
Commands must not mutate gameplay.
Commands must clearly separate active, unavailable, promised, candidate, visible, and confirmed states.

With command formats locked, the next safe step is to plan StateManager.

====================================================================================================
20_Build_Chunks_01_to_17/05_AIDRPG_Build_Chunk_05_StateManager_Plan.txt
====================================================================================================

AIDRPG Build Chunk 5 — StateManager Plan

0. Document status

Document name: AIDRPG Build Chunk 5 — StateManager Plan
Build target: AIDRPG Core MVP v0.1
Purpose: Define the exact StateManager behavior before StateManager code is generated.
Status: Practical build document.
Follows: Build Chunk 4 — Command Output Format Lock.
This document does not contain final script code.

This chunk is useful because StateManager is the first real safety foundation of the script.

If StateManager is vague, every later system becomes vague.

If StateManager repairs state incorrectly, the player can lose items, identity, quests, abilities, cards, or old progress.

If StateManager imports old prose too aggressively, old bugs return.

If StateManager does not lock startup properly, player identity can drift.

Build Chunk 5 turns the state plan into exact implementation rules.

⸻

1. StateManager doctrine

StateManager owns the shape and safety of state.aidrpg.

It does not own gameplay consequences.

The doctrine is:

Create missing structure.
Repair safe structure.
Preserve valid truth.
Quarantine unsafe old data.
Never invent gameplay truth from prose.
Never erase valid records during repair.
Never enable deferred systems by accident.

StateManager is allowed to make the save usable.

StateManager is not allowed to play the game for the player.

⸻

2. What StateManager is responsible for

StateManager owns:

* root state creation
* required bucket creation
* missing-field repair
* safe default insertion
* schema version tracking
* migration shell
* quarantine shell
* startup setup lock fields
* feature flag defaults
* structural integrity checks
* basic caps on audit/runtime arrays
* repair warnings
* safe state access helpers

StateManager does not own:

* item acquisition
* HP/MP/EP damage/healing from story events
* XP gains
* level-ups
* ability creation from gameplay
* quest activation/completion
* reward payment
* actor promotion
* location arrival
* Story Card writes
* Context packet generation
* parser decisions
* manual mutation commands

⸻

3. StateManager placement in final script

StateManager belongs in:

Section 05 — State Manager

It depends on:

Section 01 — Constants and Version
Section 02 — Feature Flags and Defaults
Section 03 — Utility Helpers
Section 04 — State Schema Defaults
Section 07 — Audit Logger, if already available or through safe warning buffer

It must not depend on:

EventScannerLite
ProposalBuilder
CanonValidator
ReducerRegistry
CardSyncLite live writes
ContextBuilderLite
CleanupManagerLite destructive cleanup

Reason:

StateManager must be foundational and safe even when later systems are still shells.

⸻

4. Required StateManager public functions

The final script should provide these StateManager functions or equivalent section-scoped functions.

StateManager.getRoot(rawState)
StateManager.init(rawState)
StateManager.createFreshRoot()
StateManager.ensureRoot(rawState)
StateManager.ensureAllBuckets(root)
StateManager.ensureBucket(root, bucketName, defaultFactory)
StateManager.ensureMeta(root)
StateManager.ensureFeatures(root)
StateManager.ensureRuntime(root)
StateManager.ensureStartup(root)
StateManager.ensurePlayer(root)
StateManager.ensureBody(root)
StateManager.ensureProgression(root)
StateManager.ensureInventory(root)
StateManager.ensureAbilities(root)
StateManager.ensureQuests(root)
StateManager.ensureActors(root)
StateManager.ensurePlaces(root)
StateManager.ensureTime(root)
StateManager.ensureCards(root)
StateManager.ensureOperationLedger(root)
StateManager.ensureAudits(root)
StateManager.ensureMigration(root)
StateManager.ensureQuarantine(root)
StateManager.ensureDirty(root)
StateManager.ensureCleanup(root)
StateManager.repairKnownFieldTypes(root)
StateManager.applySafeCaps(root)
StateManager.validateIntegrity(root)
StateManager.migrateIfNeeded(root)
StateManager.quarantine(root, record)
StateManager.addRepairWarning(root, message)
StateManager.markGameplayStarted(root)
StateManager.lockStartup(root, reason)
StateManager.isSetupLocked(root)
StateManager.getStateSummary(root)

The exact code can use shorter names, but the responsibilities must exist.

⸻

5. StateManager initialization flow

State initialization must follow this exact order.

1. Accept raw platform state.
2. Ensure platform state object exists if possible.
3. Ensure state.aidrpg root exists.
4. If state.aidrpg is missing, create fresh root.
5. If state.aidrpg exists but is malformed, quarantine preview if possible and rebuild root safely.
6. Ensure meta bucket.
7. Ensure features bucket.
8. Ensure runtime bucket.
9. Ensure startup bucket.
10. Ensure gameplay buckets.
11. Ensure cards/ledger/audits/migration/quarantine/dirty/cleanup buckets.
12. Repair safe known field types.
13. Apply safe caps to logs and arrays.
14. Run minimal migration shell if schema mismatch.
15. Validate integrity.
16. Return root.

StateManager must never skip required buckets.

StateManager must never erase valid existing buckets while adding missing ones.

⸻

6. Fresh root creation

If no AIDRPG state exists, StateManager creates a fresh root using Build Chunk 3 schema.

Fresh root must include:

meta
features
runtime
startup
player
body
progression
inventory
abilities
quests
actors
places
time
cards
operationLedger
audits
migration
quarantine
dirty
cleanup

Fresh root defaults must follow Build Chunk 3 exactly.

6.1 Fresh root must not include active deferred systems

Do not create active root buckets for:

economy
crafting
investigation
reputation
domain
absorption
warfare
multiProtagonist

These systems remain disabled through feature flags and help/releasecheck notes.

6.2 Fresh root must be safe for commands

Immediately after fresh root creation, these commands should not crash:

/help
/version
/sheet
/status
/inventory
/abilities
/quests
/actors
/location
/time
/stateaudit
/releasecheck

⸻

7. Existing root repair

If state.aidrpg already exists, StateManager must preserve it and add missing structure.

7.1 Preserve valid data

StateManager must preserve valid existing:

* player identity
* body resources
* inventory items
* equipped items
* currency
* abilities
* quests
* actors
* places
* time state
* card registry
* operation keys
* migration/quarantine records
* audits, within caps

7.2 Add missing buckets

If a bucket is missing, add only that bucket.

Example:

state.aidrpg.player exists
state.aidrpg.inventory missing

Action:

* add inventory defaults
* preserve player
* log repair warning

Do not reset the full root.

7.3 Add missing fields

If a bucket exists but lacks fields, add missing fields only.

Example:

body.hp exists
body.ep missing

Action:

* add ep default
* preserve hp/mp/injuries
* log repair warning

⸻

8. Malformed root handling

If state.aidrpg exists but is not an object:

Examples:

state.aidrpg = 'broken'
state.aidrpg = null
state.aidrpg = 7

StateManager must:

1. create a new valid root
2. add migration/quarantine warning if possible
3. store a short preview of malformed value if safe
4. never crash

Do not attempt to parse malformed prose into gameplay state.

⸻

9. Field-type repair rules

StateManager may repair safe field types.

9.1 Array fields

If an array field is missing:

set to []

If an array field is malformed:

* if recoverable, wrap or filter only safe records
* if not recoverable, quarantine preview and set empty array
* log warning

9.2 String fields

If required string field missing:

set safe default

If non-string value appears where string expected:

convert to safe string only if harmless
otherwise set default and warn

9.3 Number fields

If required number missing:

set default

If number is NaN, Infinity, or unsafe:

set default or clamp and warn

9.4 Boolean fields

If missing:

set default

If malformed:

coerce only if obvious true/false
otherwise set default and warn

9.5 Enum fields

If missing or invalid:

set safe fallback
log warning

Example:

fatigue = 'normal'
deathBoundary = 'alive'

⸻

10. Resource repair rules

Resource tracks include:

body.hp
body.mp
body.ep

Each must have:

current
max

10.1 Missing resource

If missing:

add default 100/100
warn

10.2 Current greater than max

If:

current > max

Action:

current = max
warn

10.3 Current below zero

If:

current < 0

Action:

current = 0
warn

10.4 Max below one

If:

max <= 0

Action:

max = 1 or default 100
current = clamp current
warn

10.5 Resource repair is structural, not healing

Clamping invalid numbers is repair.

It is not story healing.

Audit should make that clear.

⸻

11. Feature flag repair rules

StateManager must enforce safe MVP defaults.

11.1 Missing feature flags

Add missing flags from defaults.

11.2 Dangerous active flags

If these are true in MVP:

generatedCardsEnabled
economyEnabled
craftingEnabled
investigationEnabled
reputationEnabled
domainEnabled
absorptionEnabled
multiProtagonistEnabled
advancedCombatEnabled
manualMutationCommandsEnabled

StateManager may either:

* force them false and warn, or
* leave them but ReleaseCheck fails

MVP recommended behavior:

force generatedCardsEnabled false
force manualMutationCommandsEnabled false
force unsafe deferred flags false
warn clearly

Reason:

The first build must not accidentally activate dangerous systems from old state.

11.3 Card flags

In MVP foundation:

cardSyncDryRun = true
cardSyncSafeMode = true
protectedCardWritesEnabled = false
generatedCardsEnabled = false

⸻

12. Startup lock helpers

StateManager must provide startup lock helpers.

12.1 markGameplayStarted(root)

Sets:

startup.gameplayStarted = true

May also set:

startup.setupStatus = 'locked'
startup.setupLockedTurn = runtime.currentTurn

if setup is complete or should be locked.

12.2 lockStartup(root, reason)

Sets:

startup.setupStatus = 'locked'
startup.setupLockedTurn = runtime.currentTurn

Adds warning/note if useful.

12.3 isSetupLocked(root)

Returns true if:

startup.setupStatus = 'locked'

or:

startup.gameplayStarted = true

12.4 Lock rule

After setup lock, StateManager must not allow setup import to overwrite:

* player name
* race/species
* age
* appearance
* starting inventory
* starting abilities
* starting location
* starting companion
* starting quest

without explicit future manual override.

⸻

13. Migration shell behavior

Core MVP v0.1 needs a migration shell, not full aggressive migration.

13.1 Migration shell may do

* detect missing schema version
* detect old-looking roots
* add migration warning
* create quarantine bucket
* mark migrationStatus
* preserve current state
* ensure generated cards disabled
* ensure card sync safe/dry-run

13.2 Migration shell may not do yet

* scan every Story Card
* import old Story Card prose
* convert old card text into inventory
* convert old quest card into active quest
* convert old ability card into stable ability
* delete old cards
* neutralize old cards live
* run full old save conversion

13.3 Old roots

If old roots like these exist:

state.tasis
state.aidrpgOld
state.cardSync
state.patchData

StateManager may record warning:

Legacy-looking state detected; full migration requires later migration build step.

Do not import aggressively.

⸻

14. Quarantine helper behavior

StateManager should provide a basic quarantine helper.

14.1 Quarantine helper input

A quarantine record should include:

* source
* sourceKey
* originalPreview
* reason
* severity
* suggestedAction

14.2 Quarantine helper output

It adds to:

quarantine.records
migration.quarantined

and increments/unifies unresolved count.

14.3 Quarantine helper must not

* activate the quarantined record
* import item/quest/ability/actor
* delete the source
* resolve the record automatically

14.4 Duplicate quarantine records

If the same sourceKey/reason already exists unresolved:

* do not duplicate
* optionally update preview/turn

⸻

15. Integrity validation behavior

validateIntegrity(root) should check the state shape after initialization/repair.

15.1 Required integrity checks

Check:

* root exists
* required buckets exist
* schema version exists
* feature flags exist
* runtime fields exist
* player protected fields exist
* body resource tracks exist
* inventory separates items and sceneItems
* abilities statuses include candidate/stable
* quests rewardStatuses include promised/paid
* actors presenceStates include elsewhere/absent
* places currentLocation and travelIntent exist separately
* cards generated disabled
* operationLedger buckets exist
* audits bucket exists
* migration/quarantine exist
* dirty/cleanup exist

15.2 Integrity result

Integrity check should return a result object:

IntegrityResult {
  ok: boolean,
  warnings: string[],
  errors: string[],
  repaired: string[]
}

15.3 Integrity must not

* mutate gameplay truth beyond safe repair
* enable systems
* import prose
* run parser
* write Story Cards

⸻

16. Safe cap behavior

StateManager should apply caps to known arrays.

16.1 Must cap

runtime.hookTrace
runtime.runtimeWarnings
runtime.runtimeErrors
audits.recentProposals
audits.recentValidationResults
audits.debugMessages
audits.runtimeWarnings
audits.cardAudit
audits.contextAudit
audits.cleanupAudit
audits.migrationAudit
operationLedger.recentKeys
dirty.targets

16.2 Must not cap destructively

Do not cap/drop:

operationLedger.permanentKeys
operationLedger.rewardKeys
operationLedger.cardKeys
startup.startupOperationKeys
migration.migrationOperationKeys
important active inventory
active quests
important actors
stable abilities
protected card registry

16.3 Cap strategy

For capped arrays:

* keep most recent entries
* preserve critical errors if possible
* warn if major compaction happened

⸻

17. Repair warnings

StateManager must record repair warnings in a readable way.

17.1 Warning destinations

Repair warnings may be stored in:

audits.debugMessages
audits.runtimeWarnings
migration.warnings
state-specific warnings bucket

depending on what was repaired.

17.2 Warning style

Good:

State repair: inventory bucket was missing and has been recreated with safe defaults.

Good:

State repair: body.hp.current was greater than max and was clamped to max.

Bad:

fixed bad thing

17.3 Warning caps

Warnings must be capped to avoid bloat.

⸻

18. Preservation rules

StateManager must preserve valid truth.

18.1 Must preserve

* player name/race/age/appearance
* current resources if valid
* inventory items
* equipped records
* currency
* ability records
* quest records
* actor records
* current location
* time text/deadlines
* card registry
* operation keys
* quarantine records
* migration records
* dirty queued targets

18.2 Must not overwrite with defaults

Defaults are only for missing/unsafe fields.

Never overwrite valid values just because default factories exist.

18.3 Example

Existing:

player.name = 'Arin'

Default:

player.name = 'Unnamed Player'

Repair result:

player.name remains 'Arin'

⸻

19. Non-canon import protection

StateManager must never import gameplay truth from:

* Story Card text
* Context packets
* command output
* debug output
* old logs
* help text
* archived summaries

19.1 Example: old inventory card

Old text:

Player owns Crown of Dawn.

StateManager result:

* no Crown added
* warning or quarantine if encountered

19.2 Example: old quest card

Old text:

Active Quest: Clear the Mill Wolves.

StateManager result:

* no active quest created
* quarantine/manual review if encountered later

⸻

20. ReleaseCheck dependency

ReleaseCheckLite depends on StateManager to create/repair state enough to inspect it.

StateManager must ensure /releasecheck can run even on fresh or partially malformed state.

ReleaseCheck should fail for unsafe systems.

StateManager should not hide unsafe systems by pretending all is well.

⸻

21. Command dependency

All commands depend on StateManager.

Before a command renders, the command path should call:

StateManager.init(state)

or an equivalent safe root accessor.

This ensures commands do not crash from missing buckets.

Commands still must not mutate gameplay state beyond safe structural repair performed by StateManager.

⸻

22. Runtime dependency

Hook entry points depend on StateManager first.

Required hook order:

1. StateManager.init
2. Runtime.beginHook
3. command/context/output logic
4. Runtime.endHook

If StateManager fails, Runtime fail-soft should attempt safe return.

⸻

23. StateManager must not become a hidden patch layer

StateManager repairs structure.

It does not fix every gameplay inconsistency.

Examples of things StateManager should not silently fix:

* paying a missing reward
* completing an unfinished quest
* moving an unavailable item to inventory
* turning candidate ability into stable
* marking absent actor present
* changing player species from old card text
* deleting duplicate actors without resolver/migration plan

If such issues are detected, StateManager should warn or quarantine, not guess.

⸻

24. Required StateManager tests

BC5-001 — Fresh root creation

Input:

state.aidrpg missing

Expected:

* complete root created
* all required buckets present
* feature flags safe
* generated cards disabled

Fail if:

* missing bucket
* dangerous feature active

⸻

BC5-002 — Missing bucket repair preserves player

Input:

state.aidrpg.player.name = 'Arin'
state.aidrpg.inventory missing

Expected:

* inventory bucket added
* player name remains Arin
* repair warning logged

Fail if:

* player reset to Unnamed Player

⸻

BC5-003 — Missing EP repaired

Input:

body.hp exists
body.mp exists
body.ep missing

Expected:

* ep added as 100/100 or schema default
* hp/mp preserved
* warning logged

Fail if:

* body reset entirely

⸻

BC5-004 — Invalid resource clamped

Input:

body.hp.current = 150
body.hp.max = 100

Expected:

* hp.current = 100
* warning logged

Fail if:

* invalid value remains silently

⸻

BC5-005 — Negative resource clamped

Input:

body.mp.current = -10
body.mp.max = 100

Expected:

* mp.current = 0
* warning logged

Fail if:

* negative MP remains silently

⸻

BC5-006 — Feature flags forced safe

Input:

generatedCardsEnabled = true
craftingEnabled = true

Expected:

* generatedCardsEnabled false
* craftingEnabled false or releasecheck hard fail
* warning logged

MVP preferred:

* force false and warn

Fail if:

* dangerous flags remain active silently

⸻

BC5-007 — OperationLedger preserved

Input:

operationLedger.rewardKeys = ['quest.reward.old_bren.15_gold']

Expected:

* reward key preserved

Fail if:

* repair clears rewardKeys

⸻

BC5-008 — Quarantine record remains inactive

Input:

quarantine record says Crown of Dawn item claim

Expected:

* quarantine preserved
* no Crown added to inventory

Fail if:

* quarantine becomes active item

⸻

BC5-009 — Malformed root fails softly

Input:

state.aidrpg = 'broken'

Expected:

* new root created
* warning/quarantine preview if possible
* no crash

Fail if:

* script crashes

⸻

BC5-010 — Startup lock helper works

Input:

startup.gameplayStarted = false

Action:

markGameplayStarted
lockStartup

Expected:

* gameplayStarted true
* setupStatus locked
* setupLockedTurn set

Fail if:

* setup remains freely importable

⸻

BC5-011 — Defaults do not overwrite identity

Input:

player.name = 'Kael'
player.raceSpecies = 'elf'

Expected:

* name remains Kael
* raceSpecies remains elf

Fail if:

* defaults overwrite with Unnamed Player/human

⸻

BC5-012 — Audits capped safely

Input:

audits.debugMessages has 1000 entries

Expected:

* debugMessages capped
* no timeout
* critical recent entries preserved if possible

Fail if:

* unbounded audit remains or important recent warning removed incorrectly

⸻

BC5-013 — Old card prose not imported

Input:

Old card-like data visible to migration shell:

Player owns Crown of Dawn.

Expected:

* no Crown added
* migration warning/quarantine only if processed

Fail if:

* Crown enters inventory

⸻

BC5-014 — Required integrity fields exist after init

After initialization, verify:

* runtime.lastAppliedOutputHash exists
* runtime.suppressEventScanForCommandOutput exists
* cards.sync.generatedCardsEnabled false
* inventory.sceneItems exists
* quests.rewardStatuses includes promised and paid
* abilities.statuses includes candidate and stable

Fail if any missing.

⸻

BC5-015 — StateManager does not call forbidden systems

Review/call-graph test.

Expected:

* StateManager does not call EventScanner
* StateManager does not call ReducerRegistry
* StateManager does not call CardSync live writes
* StateManager does not parse Story Cards

Fail if StateManager becomes hidden gameplay engine.

⸻

25. StateManager acceptance checklist

StateManager Plan is complete when the implementation will clearly support:

* root creation
* bucket creation
* missing-field repair
* safe feature defaults
* runtime fields
* startup lock fields
* card safe defaults
* OperationLedger preservation
* quarantine preservation
* migration shell
* safe caps
* integrity validation
* repair warnings
* no card/context/command prose import
* no hidden gameplay mutation
* tests BC5-001 through BC5-015

⸻

* Gap Chunk E for migration/quarantine safety
* Gap Chunk G for startup lock and identity protection

Build Chunk 6 — Runtime Hook Contract Implementation Plan

* input hook flow
* context hook flow

* fail-soft behavior
