42. What Build Chunk 10 should do next

Next build document:

Build Chunk 10 — EventProposal and ProposalBuilder Plan

Build Chunk 10 should plan:

* EventProposal schema
* how ParserHits become proposals
* sourceHook preservation
* resolver result placeholders
* confidence/truth status transfer
* operation key suggestions
* proposal caps
* proposal audit
* no validation bypass
* proposal tests

Build Chunk 10 should not yet implement owner reducers.

⸻

43. Final Build Chunk 9 doctrine

The parser is a detector, not a judge.

The parser is a witness, not a reducer.

The parser can notice dangerous language, but it cannot decide truth.

Final rule:

Scan only allowed sources.
Flag danger words.
Emit ParserHits.
Never mutate state.
Never parse commands, context, cards, or audits.
Never treat player input as completed success.

With EventScannerLite planned, the next safe step is the EventProposal and ProposalBuilder plan.

====================================================================================================
20_Build_Chunks_01_to_17/10_AIDRPG_Build_Chunk_10_EventProposal_Local_Clause_Binding_and_ProposalBuilder_Plan.txt
====================================================================================================

AIDRPG Build Chunk 10 — EventProposal, Local Clause Binding, and ProposalBuilder Plan

0. Document status

Document name: AIDRPG Build Chunk 10 — EventProposal, Local Clause Binding, and ProposalBuilder Plan
Build target: AIDRPG Core MVP v0.1 / final-core architecture path
Purpose: Define the exact EventProposal schema, local clause binding rules, target binding rules, operation key suggestions, and ProposalBuilder behavior before CanonValidator and reducers are planned.
Status: Practical build document.
Follows: AIDRPG Final Build Control Map v1 and Build Chunk 9 — EventScannerLite Parser Shell Plan.
This document does not contain final script code.

This chunk exists because old versions repeatedly failed when raw parser detections were treated too close to truth.

The parser can notice a phrase.

The proposal layer must preserve what was noticed, where it was noticed, what local clause it came from, what safety flags were present, what target it appears to affect, and which owner system should judge it next.

A proposal is not canon.

A proposal is not a reducer commit.

A proposal is structured evidence waiting for CanonValidator.

⸻

1. Chunk 10 doctrine

The doctrine is:

ParserHits are observations.
EventProposals are structured claims.
CanonValidator decides eligibility.
Reducers commit truth.

The ProposalBuilder must never mutate gameplay state.

It must never decide final canon.

It must never bypass CanonValidator.

Its job is to make the dangerous middle layer exact enough that validators and reducers do not guess.

⸻

2. Why this chunk matters

Old versions had failures where positive words were enough to create state:

You decide not to take the key.
You try to grab the key, but the goblin snatches it away.
You pick up the idea of a brass key.
Mara says, "Take the key."
In a dream, you receive the Crown of Dawn.
You learned Moonblade.
The scroll title says "You learned Crimson Lotus Fang," but you learn nothing.
The road to the chapel is visible from the market lane.
"Twenty years pass," you say while practicing narration.

The parser may detect item, ability, place, or time language in those examples.

The proposal layer must preserve enough local evidence so CanonValidator can reject the false outcome.

Without local clause binding and target binding, the validator is forced to guess.

⸻

3. Placement in final script

ProposalBuilder belongs in:

Section 13 — Proposal Builder Shell

It follows:

EventScannerLite

It comes before:

CanonValidator
ReducerRegistry
OwnerReducers

It may depend on:

Constants
Utils
AuditLogger
OperationLedger key normalization helper only if read-only/suggestion based
Resolver/Sanitizer helpers for target keys only if safe

It must not depend on:

InventoryReducer
AbilityReducer
QuestReducer
BodyReducer
PlaceReducer
TimeReducer
ActorReducer
CardSync live writes
ContextBuilder mutation

⸻

4. ProposalBuilder responsibilities

ProposalBuilder owns:

* converting ParserHits into EventProposals
* preserving source hook
* preserving local clause text
* preserving safety flags
* identifying proposed event family
* identifying proposed event type
* creating target-binding candidates
* assigning owner module hint
* assigning preliminary truth status
* assigning preliminary confidence
* suggesting operation keys
* assigning proposal risk flags
* capping proposals
* auditing proposal summaries

ProposalBuilder does not own:

* canon approval
* gameplay mutation
* inventory ownership
* ability stability
* quest activation
* reward payment
* actor promotion
* location update
* time advance
* card writing
* context mutation

⸻

5. Required ProposalBuilder public functions

The final script should provide these functions or equivalents.

ProposalBuilder.fromHits(hits, root)
ProposalBuilder.fromHit(hit, root)
ProposalBuilder.buildBaseProposal(hit, root)
ProposalBuilder.bindLocalClause(hit, root)
ProposalBuilder.bindTarget(hit, family, root)
ProposalBuilder.classifyProposalFamily(hit, root)
ProposalBuilder.classifyProposalType(hit, family, root)
ProposalBuilder.assignOwner(family, eventType)
ProposalBuilder.copySafetyFlags(hit)
ProposalBuilder.suggestOperationKey(proposal, root)
ProposalBuilder.computeRiskFlags(proposal, root)
ProposalBuilder.capProposals(proposals, max)
ProposalBuilder.rejectMalformedHit(hit, reason)
ProposalBuilder.auditProposals(root, proposals, rejectedHits)

Names may change in code, but these responsibilities must exist.

⸻

6. EventProposal core schema

Every EventProposal must use a consistent schema.

EventProposal {
  proposalId: string,
  source: ProposalSource,
  family: string,
  eventType: string,
  ownerModule: string,
  localClause: LocalClauseBinding,
  target: TargetBinding,
  secondaryTargets: TargetBinding[],
  safety: SafetyFlagSet,
  evidence: ProposalEvidence,
  proposedChange: ProposedChangeSummary,
  operation: OperationSuggestion,
  confidence: number,
  truthStatus: string,
  riskFlags: string[],
  validatorRequired: boolean,
  reducerRequired: boolean,
  status: 'proposed' | 'malformed' | 'blocked_prevalidation' | 'deferred',
  proposalNotes: string[]
}

No proposal should be reduced directly.

All proposals require CanonValidator unless explicitly marked malformed and discarded.

⸻

7. ProposalSource schema

ProposalSource {
  sourceHook: 'input' | 'output' | 'manual' | 'migration' | 'setup',
  parserHitId: string,
  inputHash: string,
  outputHash: string,
  textHash: string,
  turn: number,
  commandSuppressed: boolean,
  duplicateOutput: boolean,
  nonCanonSource: boolean,
  sourceLabel: string
}

7.1 Required source rule

If source is command, context, story card, debug, audit, help, releasecheck, or unknown non-canon source, ProposalBuilder must not create a gameplay proposal.

The parser should already reject those sources, but ProposalBuilder must treat this as a second safety layer.

7.2 Input source rule

Input proposals are intent/attempt proposals only unless they come from an explicit startup/setup/manual-confirmed path.

Normal player input does not create completed success.

7.3 Output source rule

Output proposals may describe possible completed consequences, but they are still not canon.

⸻

8. LocalClauseBinding schema

Local clause binding is required for every proposal.

LocalClauseBinding {
  fullWindowPreview: string,
  localClauseText: string,
  localClauseHash: string,
  clauseIndex: number,
  sentenceIndex: number,
  beforeTextPreview: string,
  afterTextPreview: string,
  matchedText: string,
  matchedTextHash: string,
  clauseRole: 'main' | 'subordinate' | 'quoted' | 'conditional' | 'unknown',
  denialScope: string,
  revocationScope: string,
  targetScope: string,
  notes: string[]
}

8.1 Why local clause binding exists

Local clause binding prevents broad false decisions.

Example:

Mira refuses to give you the jeweled crown, but she hands you the red healing potion.

The proposal layer must create separate local target proposals:

crown -> local clause has refusal/denial
red healing potion -> local clause has handoff confirmation

If it produces one broad proposal for the whole sentence, the validator cannot safely decide.

⸻

9. TargetBinding schema

Every proposal must bind a target or explicitly state that target is unknown.

TargetBinding {
  targetFamily: 'item' | 'ability' | 'quest' | 'reward' | 'actor' | 'place' | 'time' | 'body' | 'status' | 'progression' | 'card' | 'unknown',
  rawTargetText: string,
  cleanedTargetText: string,
  canonicalKey: string,
  displayName: string,
  targetId: string,
  targetStatus: 'known' | 'candidate' | 'new_candidate' | 'ambiguous' | 'generic' | 'reference_only' | 'unknown',
  targetConfidence: number,
  sanitizerUsed: string,
  resolverNotes: string[]
}

9.1 Owner-specific sanitizer rule

TargetBinding must use the correct sanitizer family.

Examples:

AbilityNameSanitizer for ability targets
ItemNameSanitizer for item targets
PlaceNameSanitizer for place targets
ActorNameSanitizer for actor targets
QuestObjectiveSanitizer for quest/objective targets
CardNameSanitizer for card targets

Shared cleaners may normalize spacing and punctuation only.

They must not perform destructive family-specific stripping.

⸻

10. SafetyFlagSet schema

Every proposal must carry all parser safety flags forward.

SafetyFlagSet {
  negated: boolean,
  hypothetical: boolean,
  quotedSpeech: boolean,
  dreamOrVision: boolean,
  rumorOrClaim: boolean,
  failedOrRevoked: boolean,
  conditional: boolean,
  futureIntent: boolean,
  attemptOnly: boolean,
  commandOutput: boolean,
  contextText: boolean,
  storyCardText: boolean,
  debugOrAuditText: boolean,
  abstractOrMetaphorical: boolean,
  visibleOnly: boolean,
  availableOnly: boolean,
  deniedByLocalClause: boolean,
  revokedByLocalClause: boolean,
  nonliteral: boolean,
  sourceUnsafe: boolean,
  confidencePenaltyReasons: string[]
}

10.1 Safety flags are sticky

ProposalBuilder may add safety flags.

It must not erase parser safety flags.

If a hit was dream/vision, quoted, negated, failed, or hypothetical, the proposal must carry that forward.

⸻

11. ProposalEvidence schema

ProposalEvidence {
  rawTextPreview: string,
  normalizedTextPreview: string,
  matchedTextPreview: string,
  evidenceType: 'input_intent' | 'output_consequence' | 'setup_structured' | 'migration_structural' | 'manual_future' | 'parser_shell',
  sourceConfidence: number,
  parserConfidence: number,
  proposalConfidence: number,
  supportPhrases: string[],
  blockingPhrases: string[],
  ambiguityPhrases: string[],
  notes: string[]
}

Evidence is not canon.

It is only the explanation package for CanonValidator.

⸻

12. ProposedChangeSummary schema

The proposed change summary should describe what would happen if CanonValidator and owner reducer accepted it.

ProposedChangeSummary {
  action: string,
  targetFamily: string,
  targetKey: string,
  targetDisplayName: string,
  quantity: number,
  direction: 'gain' | 'loss' | 'update' | 'resolve' | 'observe' | 'none' | 'unknown',
  currentStateHint: string,
  proposedStateHint: string,
  reversible: boolean,
  repeatSensitive: boolean,
  dirtyFamiliesIfAccepted: string[],
  notes: string[]
}

ProposalBuilder does not apply this change.

It only describes it.

⸻

13. OperationSuggestion schema

Operation suggestions help later validators/reducers/ledger create stable dedupe keys.

OperationSuggestion {
  suggestedKey: string,
  keyFamily: 'recent' | 'permanent' | 'reward' | 'card' | 'sourceUse' | 'none',
  repeatSensitive: boolean,
  durable: boolean,
  reason: string,
  requiredBeforeCommit: boolean,
  notes: string[]
}

13.1 Key suggestion rule

ProposalBuilder may suggest a key.

OperationLedger commits keys only after validation/reducer flow approves or skips according to owner policy.

ProposalBuilder must not add ledger keys for gameplay effects by itself.

⸻

14. Proposal family map

Initial supported proposal families:

inventory
ability
body_status
body_injury
quest
reward
actor
place_scene
time
progression
card_mirror
migration
unknown

Each family has a required owner module.

inventory -> InventoryReducer
ability -> AbilityReducer
body_status/body_injury -> BodyReducer
quest -> QuestReducerLite
reward -> QuestReducerLite / RewardValidator
actor -> ActorReducerLite
place_scene -> PlaceReducerLite
time -> TimeReducerLite
progression -> ProgressionReducerLite
card_mirror -> CardSyncLite, not gameplay canon
migration -> MigrationManager, not gameplay canon unless structurally safe
unknown -> no reducer, audit only

⸻

15. Inventory proposal rules

Inventory proposals must never be built as direct grants from broad phrases.

15.1 Inventory proposal event types

inventory.visible_candidate
inventory.available_loot_candidate
inventory.acquire_attempt
inventory.acquire_candidate
inventory.handoff_candidate
inventory.transfer_candidate
inventory.drop_candidate
inventory.lose_candidate
inventory.consume_candidate
inventory.destroy_candidate
inventory.denied_candidate
inventory.revoked_candidate
inventory.abstract_reference

15.2 Inventory target binding

Inventory proposals must bind:

item target
local clause
source actor if present
target custody hint if present
acquisition classification

15.3 Required blocked inventory proposal cases

ProposalBuilder must mark these as blocked/risky before validation:

never take X
decide not to take X
fail to take X
try to grab X but someone snatches it
idea/concept/title of X
pretend to take X
in a dream/vision receive X
quoted "take X"
dissolves as illusion
wake empty-handed
have nothing
refuses to give X

15.4 Required allowed inventory proposal cases

ProposalBuilder may create acquire/handoff candidates for:

Mira hands you the red healing potion.
She places the key in your hand and you accept it.
You recover the black gate key after searching again.
You pick up the rusty dagger.
You receive the rusted iron key.

Still requires CanonValidator.

15.5 Inventory operation key suggestions

Suggested patterns:

item.acquire.<itemKey>.<sourceOrScene>.<localClauseHash>
item.handoff.<itemKey>.<fromActor>.<toActor>.<localClauseHash>
item.consume.<itemKey>.<localClauseHash>
item.drop.<itemKey>.<localClauseHash>

⸻

16. Ability proposal rules

Ability proposals must distinguish training, attempt, manifestation, failure, stability, and mention.

16.1 Ability proposal event types

ability.train_intent
ability.train_candidate
ability.use_attempt
ability.use_success_candidate
ability.use_failure_candidate
ability.learn_candidate
ability.stabilize_candidate
ability.evidence_candidate
ability.quoted_reference
ability.pretend_reference
ability.failed_reference
ability.output_only_learning_claim

16.2 Ability name binding

Ability proposals must use AbilityNameSanitizer.

Examples:

I train Ember Ward to protect allies from heat -> Ember Ward
I practice Frost Step by moving across slick stones -> Frost Step
I drill Stoneguard until my stance holds under impact -> Stoneguard
I train Moonblade by practicing careful crescent cuts -> Moonblade

16.3 Explicit training priority

If the local clause begins with:

train/practice/drill/work on/refine/meditate on/spar with + named phrase

then the proposal family is ability training even if purpose words include:

protect/shield/defend/against/for/from

16.4 Output-only ability learning safety

Output-only learning claims must be proposed as risky, not canon-ready.

Examples:

You learned Moonblade.
You unlocked Star Chain.
New skill: Mirror Guard.

Proposal type:

ability.output_only_learning_claim

CanonValidator will normally reject or downgrade unless supported by training/source context.

16.5 Blocked ability proposal cases

quoted "Use Hollow Star Palm"
pretend to use Azure Dragon Step
attempt Void Lantern Veil, but it collapses
scroll title says "you learned X" but learn nothing
do not know X
no usable technique remains
X fizzles
X cannot manifest

16.6 Ability operation key suggestions

ability.train.<abilityKey>.<inputHashOrClauseHash>
ability.use.<abilityKey>.<eventKind>.<outputHashOrClauseHash>
ability.evidence.<abilityKey>.<evidenceKind>.<clauseHash>
ability.stabilize.<abilityKey>.<sourceKey>

⸻

17. Body/status proposal rules

Body proposals must distinguish status gain, status loss, injury, recovery, death boundary, and resource state.

17.1 Body event types

body.status_gain_candidate
body.status_loss_candidate
body.injury_candidate
body.resource_state_candidate
body.resource_change_candidate
body.recovery_candidate
body.death_boundary_candidate
body.death_rumor_or_quote

17.2 Status proposal examples

Your legs shake and you are exhausted by the climb -> exhausted status gain, EP low/empty candidate
You are fatigued and worn out -> fatigued status gain, not exhausted
Your palm is burned and blistered -> burned status/injury candidate
The bleeding stops -> bleeding status loss candidate

17.3 Death boundary safety

ProposalBuilder must mark these as non-canon/risky:

People say you died.
Mara says, "you are dead."
In a dream, you die.
You feel dead tired.

It may create a death-boundary candidate only for direct confirmed severe outcome wording.

⸻

18. Quest and reward proposal rules

Quest and reward proposals must be separate.

18.1 Quest event types

quest.rumor_candidate
quest.offer_candidate
quest.accept_intent
quest.accept_candidate
quest.objective_progress_candidate
quest.objective_complete_candidate
quest.ready_to_turn_in_candidate
quest.failed_candidate
quest.abandoned_candidate

18.2 Reward event types

reward.promise_candidate
reward.ready_candidate
reward.payment_candidate
reward.denied_candidate
reward.disputed_candidate
reward.output_only_claim

18.3 Objective verb breadth

ProposalBuilder should preserve objective text for common verbs:

recover
find
repair
escort
clear
deliver
investigate
protect
bring
retrieve
rescue
defeat
survey

18.4 Promise/payment separation

Old Bren promises to pay you 15 gold -> reward.promise_candidate
Old Bren pays you 15 gold -> reward.payment_candidate

Promise never becomes payment at proposal stage.

18.5 Reward operation key suggestions

quest.accept.<questKey>.<giverKey>
quest.objective.<questKey>.<objectiveKey>.<completionHash>
quest.reward.<questKey>.<payerKey>.<rewardKey>
xp.quest.<questKey>.<sourceKey>

⸻

19. Place/scene proposal rules

Place proposals must separate current scene confirmation, strong arrival, weak mention, future intent, visible/nearby reference, and route/interstitial.

19.1 Place event types

place.current_scene_confirm_candidate
place.arrival_candidate
place.travel_intent_candidate
place.visible_reference
place.nearby_reference
place.future_reference
place.route_reference
place.weak_mention

19.2 Current scene confirmation examples

The market lane remains busy.
You remain in the market lane.
The market square buzzes with merchants.
You stand in the market lane.

19.3 Weak mention examples

chapel visible from here
down the street
nearby
beyond the road
only consider going
someone mentions the chapel

These proposals must not suggest current scene overwrite.

19.4 Strong arrival examples

You enter the chapel.
You walk inside the smithy.
You descend into the cellar.
After the road, you enter The Drunken Huntsman.

Strong arrival proposals bind the named destination, not the route/interstitial word.

19.5 Place operation key suggestions

place.scene.confirm.<placeKey>.<clauseHash>
place.arrive.<placeKey>.<clauseHash>
place.intent.<placeKey>.<inputHash>

⸻

20. Time and aging proposal rules

Time proposals must separate literal time passage from quoted, dreamed, pretend, nonliteral, or narrative-practice references.

20.1 Time event types

time.advance_candidate
time.age_candidate
time.deadline_candidate
time.nonliteral_reference
time.quoted_reference
time.dream_reference
time.no_time_passes_reference

20.2 Blocked time cases

"Twenty years pass," you say.
In a dream, twenty years pass.
You practice narration: twenty years pass.
No time actually passes.
You wake as the same age.

20.3 Allowed time cases

Twenty years pass in exile.
After two years, the city rebuilds.
A week passes during the journey.

Still requires CanonValidator and owner reducer.

20.4 Time operation key suggestions

time.advance.<amountKey>.<clauseHash>
time.age.<amountKey>.<clauseHash>
time.deadline.<deadlineKey>.<sourceKey>

⸻

21. Actor proposal rules

Actor proposals must separate present actors, scene-only actors, absent/reference-only actors, rumored actors, and relationship/claim mentions.

21.1 Actor event types

actor.present_candidate
actor.scene_only_candidate
actor.reference_only_candidate
actor.absent_candidate
actor.memory_candidate
actor.relationship_claim_candidate
actor.status_candidate
actor.death_candidate

21.2 Compound actor suppression handoff

ProposalBuilder should preserve resolver notes if compound-shorter suppression occurred.

Example:

A veteran guard stands nearby.

Expected target binding:

Veteran Guard
not separate Guard

21.3 Absent/reference-only safety

the goblin is definitely not here -> actor.absent_candidate/reference_only, no present actor
training dummy named after Mira -> reference target, not actor Mira harm
Mara elsewhere -> no current-scene action

⸻

22. Progression proposal rules

Progression proposals are conservative in MVP.

22.1 Progression event types

progression.xp_gain_candidate
progression.level_up_candidate
progression.stat_gain_candidate
progression.training_progress_candidate
progression.output_only_claim

22.2 Explicit XP only

Output:

You gain 20 XP.

may produce XP proposal.

Output:

This was a learning experience.

must not produce XP proposal.

22.3 Operation key suggestions

xp.gain.<amount>.<sourceKey>.<clauseHash>
stat.gain.<statKey>.<amount>.<sourceKey>
level.up.<fromTo>.<sourceKey>

⸻

23. Card and migration proposal rules

Card and migration proposals are not gameplay canon proposals.

23.1 Card proposal types

card.protected_update_candidate
card.generated_create_blocked
card.duplicate_detected
card.tombstone_candidate
card.manual_edit_detected

These route to CardSyncLite, not gameplay reducers.

23.2 Migration proposal types

migration.structural_import_candidate
migration.quarantine_candidate
migration.repair_candidate
migration.ignore_candidate

Migration proposals must never import old card prose as gameplay truth.

⸻

24. Proposal risk flags

ProposalBuilder should compute risk flags for validators and debug output.

Required risk flags:

SOURCE_NON_CANON
INPUT_ATTEMPT_ONLY
OUTPUT_ONLY_UNSUPPORTED
NEGATED_LOCAL_CLAUSE
REVOKED_LOCAL_CLAUSE
FAILED_ACTION
ABSTRACT_REFERENCE
QUOTED_SPEECH
DREAM_OR_VISION
RUMOR_OR_CLAIM
HYPOTHETICAL_OR_CONDITIONAL
FUTURE_INTENT_ONLY
VISIBLE_NOT_OWNED
AVAILABLE_NOT_OWNED
PROMISED_NOT_PAID
WEAK_SCENE_REFERENCE
ROUTE_INTERSTITIAL_RISK
ABSENT_ACTOR_RISK
REFERENCE_ONLY_ACTOR_RISK
NONLITERAL_TIME_RISK
DUPLICATE_SENSITIVE
TARGET_AMBIGUOUS
SANITIZER_LOW_CONFIDENCE
UNKNOWN_OWNER

Risk flags do not automatically reject.

They inform CanonValidator.

Some flags should usually force reject for specific families.

⸻

25. Proposal status meanings

proposed

Means the proposal is structurally valid and needs CanonValidator.

malformed

Means the parser hit lacked necessary structure and should be audited/skipped.

blocked_prevalidation

Means source was non-canon or proposal is obviously impossible before validation.

deferred

Means event belongs to a disabled system such as economy, crafting, investigation, domain, generated cards, or advanced combat.

⸻

26. Malformed hit handling

ProposalBuilder must not crash on malformed ParserHits.

If a hit is missing required fields:

* create no gameplay proposal
* record rejected/malformed summary
* audit if useful
* continue processing other hits

Examples of malformed hits:

missing sourceHook
missing eventFamily
no local clause or matched text
unsafe source
no target for target-required family

⸻

27. Proposal caps

Recommended caps:

maxProposalsPerOutput = 20
maxProposalsPerInput = 10
maxMalformedHitAuditsPerTurn = 5
maxProposalAuditLinesPerTurn = 10
maxSecondaryTargetsPerProposal = 5
maxLocalClausePreviewLength = 180

If proposals exceed caps:

* keep current-stage mandatory families first
* prefer high-confidence, direct-output consequences
* preserve rejection/audit for skipped high-risk items if possible
* do not crash
* do not scan history to compensate

⸻

28. Proposal audit format

Proposal audit should be concise.

Example:

Proposal: inventory.acquire_candidate target=red_healing_potion source=output risk=DUPLICATE_SENSITIVE confidence=0.74.

Example:

Proposal blocked prevalidation: inventory.acquire_candidate target=jeweled_crown reason=local clause refusal.

Example:

Proposal: ability.train_candidate target=ember_ward source=input risk=INPUT_ATTEMPT_ONLY confidence=0.62.

Do not dump full raw text.

⸻

29. ProposalBuilder and CanonValidator handoff

Every structurally valid proposal must go to CanonValidator.

Handoff object:

{
  proposals: EventProposal[],
  malformedHits: RejectedHitSummary[],
  skippedBySource: number,
  skippedByCap: number,
  auditSummary: string[]
}

CanonValidator must receive all safety flags, local clause binding, target binding, proposed change summary, and operation suggestion.

⸻

30. ProposalBuilder and OperationLedger relationship

ProposalBuilder may suggest keys.

It must not commit keys for gameplay effects.

Exceptions:

* source-use audit keys may be handled by Runtime or OperationLedger outside ProposalBuilder
* ProposalBuilder should not add permanent/reward/card keys

Reducers or commit controller later add keys after validation according to owner policy.

⸻

31. ProposalBuilder and commands/context/cards

ProposalBuilder must not build proposals from:

[AIDRPG] command output
context packets
Story Card text
protected card text
generated card text
debug output
audit output
help output
releasecheck output
old logs

If such text reaches ProposalBuilder, it should mark source unsafe and return blocked/prevalidation skip.

⸻

32. ProposalBuilder and disabled systems

If a ParserHit points to a disabled/deferred system, ProposalBuilder may create a deferred diagnostic proposal but not a gameplay proposal.

Deferred systems in MVP:

economy
crafting
investigation
reputation automation
domain/labor
absorption/evolution
advanced combat
party/summons advanced controls
multi-protagonist
warfare/group combat
generated cards
manual mutation commands

Deferred proposal status:

deferred

ReducerRequired:

false

ValidatorRequired:

true or diagnostic-only depending releasecheck needs

⸻

33. Required proposal tests

BC10-001 — ParserHit does not mutate state

Given ParserHit for:

You pick up the rusty dagger.

Expected:

* EventProposal may be created
* inventory unchanged

Fail if ProposalBuilder adds item.

⸻

BC10-002 — Proposal carries source hook

Given output ParserHit.

Expected:

proposal.source.sourceHook = output

Fail if source missing.

⸻

BC10-003 — Input proposal is attempt-only

Input:

I take the jeweled crown.

Expected:

* inventory acquire attempt proposal
* attemptOnly true
* completed outcome not assumed

Fail if input proposal marked confirmed acquisition.

⸻

BC10-004 — Command output blocked

Text:

[AIDRPG] Inventory
- Rusty Dagger

Expected:

* no gameplay EventProposal
* source unsafe or blocked prevalidation

Fail if item proposal created.

⸻

BC10-005 — Context text blocked

Source:

context

Text:

The Iron Sword is in Mara's custody.

Expected:

* no gameplay EventProposal

Fail if custody/inventory proposal created.

⸻

BC10-006 — Story Card text blocked

Source:

story_card

Text:

Player owns Crown of Dawn.

Expected:

* no gameplay EventProposal

Fail if inventory proposal created.

⸻

BC10-007 — Inventory denial local clause

Output:

You decide not to take the rusted iron key.

Expected:

* proposal target rusted iron key
* safety deniedByLocalClause true or eventType denied_candidate
* proposed change does not say gain inventory

Fail if acquire_candidate without denial risk.

⸻

BC10-008 — Inventory failed grab local clause

Output:

You try to grab the rusted iron key, but the goblin snatches it away first.

Expected:

* rusted iron key proposal marked failed/revoked
* no acquire-ready proposal
* item name does not include but the goblin

Fail if target becomes rusted iron key but the goblin or acquire-ready.

⸻

BC10-009 — Mixed clause inventory binding

Output:

Mira refuses to give you the jeweled crown, but she hands you the red healing potion.

Expected:

* crown proposal blocked/denied
* red healing potion handoff candidate allowed for validation
* separate local clauses/targets

Fail if crown and potion collapse into one proposal.

⸻

BC10-010 — Abstract item blocked

Output:

You pick up the idea of a brass key.

Expected:

* abstractOrMetaphorical true
* no acquire-ready proposal

Fail if brass key proposal looks confirmed.

⸻

BC10-011 — Dream item blocked

Output:

In a dream, you receive the Crown of Dawn, then wake empty-handed.

Expected:

* dreamOrVision true
* revoked/empty-handed risk true
* no acquire-ready proposal

Fail if Crown proposal lacks dream/revoked risk.

⸻

BC10-012 — Ability training priority

Input:

I train Ember Ward to protect allies from heat.

Expected:

* ability.train_intent or train_candidate
* target Ember Ward
* not defend-only

Fail if no ability proposal.

⸻

BC10-013 — Ability name cleanup owner-specific

Input:

I practice Frost Step by moving across slick stones.

Expected:

* target display Frost Step
* sanitizerUsed AbilityNameSanitizer

Fail if target is Frost Step by moving across slick stones.

⸻

BC10-014 — Output-only learned ability risky

Output:

You learned Moonblade.

Expected:

* ability.output_only_learning_claim
* risk OUTPUT_ONLY_UNSUPPORTED
* not stable canon

Fail if proposal type is stable/confirmed without risk.

⸻

BC10-015 — Failed ability blocked

Output:

You attempt Void Lantern Veil, but the veil collapses and no usable technique remains.

Expected:

* ability failure proposal
* failedOrRevoked true
* no stabilize proposal

Fail if stabilize/learn proposal created.

⸻

BC10-016 — Quoted ability blocked

Output:

Mira says, "Use Hollow Star Palm now," but you do not know that technique.

Expected:

* quotedSpeech true
* failed/unknown technique risk
* no stable ability proposal

Fail if ability learned/use-success proposal created.

⸻

BC10-017 — Exhausted proposal

Output:

Your legs shake and you are exhausted by the climb.

Expected:

* body.status_gain_candidate exhausted
* possible resource_state_candidate EP low/empty
* severity hint severe

Fail if no body/status proposal.

⸻

BC10-018 — Fatigue not exhaustion

Output:

You are fatigued and worn out from the long march.

Expected:

* fatigued candidate
* not exhausted unless explicit exhausted-level wording

Fail if exhausted proposal created from mild fatigue only.

⸻

BC10-019 — Weak scene mention

Output:

The road to the chapel is visible from the market lane, but you only consider heading there.

Expected:

* chapel proposal type weak/visible/future reference
* no arrival/current-scene proposal for chapel

Fail if chapel arrival/current-scene proposal created.

⸻

BC10-020 — Scene remains confirmation

Output:

The market lane remains busy with merchants and passing townsfolk.

Expected:

* current_scene_confirm_candidate market lane

Fail if only weak hint with no scene confirmation proposal.

⸻

BC10-021 — Strong named arrival beats road

Output:

After the road, you enter The Drunken Huntsman.

Expected:

* arrival proposal target The Drunken Huntsman
* route/interstitial risk for road, but not target

Fail if scene target road.

⸻

BC10-022 — Nonliteral time quote blocked

Output:

You say, "Twenty years pass," while practicing narration. No time passes.

Expected:

* time.nonliteral_reference or quoted/no-time-passes risk
* no age_candidate ready for validation as literal

Fail if literal age proposal created.

⸻

BC10-023 — Literal time pass proposal

Output:

Twenty years pass in exile.

Expected:

* time.advance_candidate and possible time.age_candidate
* literal, but still requires validation

Fail if no proposal in active parser mode.

⸻

BC10-024 — Reward promise not payment

Output:

Old Bren promises to pay you 15 gold if you clear the wolves.

Expected:

* reward.promise_candidate
* conditional/futureIntent true
* no payment_candidate

Fail if payment proposal created.

⸻

BC10-025 — Reward payment candidate

Output:

Old Bren pays you the promised 15 gold.

Expected:

* reward.payment_candidate
* repeatSensitive true
* suggested reward key

Fail if proposal lacks repeat-sensitive operation suggestion.

⸻

BC10-026 — Actor compound suppression carried

Output:

A veteran guard stands near the gate.

Expected:

* actor target Veteran Guard
* no separate Guard proposal for same span

Fail if duplicate actor proposals created.

⸻

BC10-027 — Absent actor reference-only

Output:

The goblin is definitely not here.

Expected:

* actor absent/reference-only proposal or no present actor proposal
* no actor.present_candidate

Fail if present goblin proposal created.

⸻

BC10-028 — Body/death rumor blocked

Output:

People say you died in the ruins.

Expected:

* death rumor/claim risk
* no direct death boundary candidate

Fail if death candidate created without rumor risk.

⸻

BC10-029 — Proposal cap enforced

Output contains many repeated candidate events.

Expected:

* proposals capped
* skipped count/audit exists
* no crash

Fail if unbounded proposals.

⸻

BC10-030 — Proposal audit compact

After proposal building.

Expected:

* audit summaries compact
* no full giant raw text dump

Fail if full output copied repeatedly into audits.

⸻

34. ReleaseCheck requirements for Build Chunk 10

ReleaseCheck must eventually verify:

EventProposal schema exists
local clause binding exists
target binding exists
source hook preservation exists
safety flags carry over from ParserHit
owner module assignment exists
operation key suggestion exists for repeat-sensitive families
non-canon sources blocked before proposal
input proposals attempt-only
inventory/ability/time/place/reward proposal risk flags exist
proposal caps exist
proposal audit exists

ReleaseCheck must fail if:

ParserHit can become reducer action without EventProposal
EventProposal lacks local clause binding
EventProposal lacks safety flags
ProposalBuilder creates gameplay state
ProposalBuilder builds proposals from command/context/card/audit/help text
input proposal marks completed success from player intent alone
inventory proposal cannot represent denied/revoked/abstract/dream/quote cases
ability proposal cannot represent output-only unsupported learning or failed/quoted ability cases
place proposal cannot separate weak mention from strong arrival
time proposal cannot represent nonliteral/quoted/dream references
reward proposal cannot separate promise from payment

⸻

⸻

37. Final Build Chunk 10 doctrine

The proposal layer is where raw text becomes structured evidence.

It must preserve danger instead of smoothing it away.

Final rule:

Bind the source.
Bind the local clause.
Bind the target.
Carry every safety flag.
Suggest, do not commit.
Route to the owner, do not mutate.
Make CanonValidator powerful enough to reject false canon.

With EventProposal and ProposalBuilder planned, the next safe step is CanonValidator.

====================================================================================================
20_Build_Chunks_01_to_17/11_AIDRPG_Build_Chunk_11_CanonValidator_and_Clause_Level_Canon_Rules_Plan.txt
====================================================================================================

AIDRPG Build Chunk 11 — CanonValidator and Clause-Level Canon Rules Plan

0. Document status

Document name: AIDRPG Build Chunk 11 — CanonValidator and Clause-Level Canon Rules Plan
Build target: AIDRPG Core MVP v0.1 / final-core architecture path
Purpose: Define the exact CanonValidator behavior, clause-level canon rules, family-specific accept/reject logic, audit format, and validation tests before reducers are planned.
Status: Practical build document.
Follows: AIDRPG Final Build Control Map v1 and Build Chunk 10 — EventProposal, Local Clause Binding, and ProposalBuilder Plan.
This document does not contain final script code.

This chunk exists because the old scripts repeatedly failed when parser hits, output phrasing, old card text, command text, or broad patches were allowed to become truth too easily.

The CanonValidator is the central truth gate.

It decides whether an EventProposal is:

accepted
rejected
pending
deferred
malformed
blocked

CanonValidator does not mutate gameplay state.

Reducers mutate state only after CanonValidator accepts a proposal.

⸻

1. CanonValidator doctrine

The core doctrine is:

Mentioned is not confirmed.
Attempted is not confirmed.
Quoted is not confirmed.
Dreamed is not confirmed.
Rumored is not confirmed.
Revoked is not retained.
Denied is not acquired.
Visible is not owned.
Promised is not paid.
Heading toward is not arrival.
Candidate is not stable.
Only validated outcome becomes canon.

CanonValidator protects the system from false canon.

It must be conservative by default.

⸻

2. Why CanonValidator is required

Old versions showed that normal parser and command behavior is not enough.

The system must reject cases like:

You decide not to take the rusted iron key.
You try to grab the key, but the goblin snatches it away.
You pick up the idea of a brass key.
Mara says, "Take the key."
In a dream, you receive the Crown of Dawn.
You learned Moonblade.
The scroll title says "You learned Crimson Lotus Fang," but you learn nothing.
The chapel is visible from here.
"Twenty years pass," you say while practicing narration.
People say you died.

The parser can detect those as relevant.

ProposalBuilder can structure them.

CanonValidator decides they are not canon outcomes.

⸻

3. Placement in final script

CanonValidator belongs in:

Section 14 — Canon Validator Shell

It follows:

EventScannerLite
ProposalBuilder

It comes before:

ReducerRegistry
OwnerReducers
DirtyQueue
CardSyncLite
ContextBuilderLite

It may depend on:

Constants
Utils
AuditLogger
OperationLedger read checks
Resolver read helpers
State read helpers

It must not depend on:

InventoryReducer
AbilityReducer
QuestReducer
BodyReducer
PlaceReducer
TimeReducer
ActorReducer
CardSync live writes
ContextBuilder mutation

⸻

4. CanonValidator responsibilities

CanonValidator owns:

* validating EventProposals
* rejecting non-canon sources
* applying clause-level canon rules
* applying family-specific canon rules
* checking operation-ledger duplicates before commit when relevant
* assigning validation result status
* explaining rejection/acceptance/pending/deferred reasons
* producing validation audits
* refusing unsafe deferred systems
* protecting reducer boundaries

CanonValidator does not own:

* parser detection
* proposal construction
* gameplay state mutation
* card writing
* context generation
* cleanup deletion
* command rendering

⸻

5. Required CanonValidator public functions

The final script should provide these functions or equivalents.

CanonValidator.validateAll(proposals, root)
CanonValidator.validate(proposal, root)
CanonValidator.validateSource(proposal, root)
CanonValidator.validateClause(proposal, root)
CanonValidator.validateTarget(proposal, root)
CanonValidator.validateFamily(proposal, root)
CanonValidator.validateInventory(proposal, root)
CanonValidator.validateAbility(proposal, root)
CanonValidator.validateBody(proposal, root)
CanonValidator.validateQuest(proposal, root)
CanonValidator.validateReward(proposal, root)
CanonValidator.validateActor(proposal, root)
CanonValidator.validatePlaceScene(proposal, root)
CanonValidator.validateTime(proposal, root)
CanonValidator.validateProgression(proposal, root)
CanonValidator.validateCardMirror(proposal, root)
CanonValidator.validateMigration(proposal, root)
CanonValidator.checkDuplicateRisk(proposal, root)
CanonValidator.accept(proposal, reason, notes)
CanonValidator.reject(proposal, reason, notes)
CanonValidator.pending(proposal, reason, notes)
CanonValidator.defer(proposal, reason, notes)
CanonValidator.block(proposal, reason, notes)
CanonValidator.auditResults(root, results)

Names may change in code, but these responsibilities must exist.

⸻

6. ValidationResult schema

Every validation returns a structured result.

ValidationResult {
  proposalId: string,
  family: string,
  eventType: string,
  ownerModule: string,
  targetKey: string,
  targetDisplayName: string,
  status: 'accepted' | 'rejected' | 'pending' | 'deferred' | 'blocked' | 'malformed' | 'duplicate_skipped',
  canonAllowed: boolean,
  reducerAllowed: boolean,
  operationKey: string,
  duplicate: boolean,
  reasons: string[],
  requiredReducer: string,
  dirtyFamiliesIfAccepted: string[],
  auditLine: string,
  source: ProposalSource,
  localClause: LocalClauseBinding,
  safety: SafetyFlagSet,
  notes: string[]
}

No reducer may apply a proposal unless:

status = accepted
canonAllowed = true
reducerAllowed = true
requiredReducer is known

⸻

7. Validation status meanings

7.1 accepted

The proposal may be routed to its owner reducer.

Accepted does not mean already committed.

7.2 rejected

The proposal is not canon and must not mutate state.

7.3 pending

The proposal is plausible but lacks enough confirmation.

Pending may become evidence, rumor, hint, candidate, or audit depending family.

7.4 deferred

The proposal belongs to a disabled system or future mini-spec.

7.5 blocked

The proposal is unsafe because of source, command output, card text, context text, debug text, or forbidden path.

7.6 malformed

The proposal is structurally invalid.

7.7 duplicate_skipped

The proposal appears valid but a durable/recent operation key shows it was already applied or should not repeat.

⸻

8. Universal source validation

CanonValidator must reject or block unsafe sources before family-specific logic.

Blocked sources:

command output
context text
Story Card text
protected card text
generated card text
debug output
audit output
help output
releasecheck output
old logs
unknown non-canon source

Allowed sources in MVP:

input as intent/attempt only
output as possible consequence
setup structured source before lock
migration structural source only if safe
manual future source only after future spec

8.1 Command output rule

Any proposal from text beginning with:

[AIDRPG]

must be blocked unless it is diagnostic-only and never routed to gameplay reducers.

8.2 Context rule

Context may remind the AI of state.

Context may not create, update, or repair gameplay canon.

8.3 Card rule

Story Cards mirror state.

Story Card text does not create canon.

⸻

9. Universal clause validation

CanonValidator must evaluate local clause safety before family-specific acceptance.

Universal rejecting or downgrading flags:

negated
hypothetical
quotedSpeech
dreamOrVision
rumorOrClaim
failedOrRevoked
conditional
futureIntent
attemptOnly
abstractOrMetaphorical
deniedByLocalClause
revokedByLocalClause
nonliteral
sourceUnsafe

These flags do not always reject every family, but they must affect validation.

Example:

* quoted speech may create an actor memory claim.
* quoted speech may not create inventory acquisition.
* dream may create dream memory.
* dream may not create real item ownership.
* future intent may create travel intent.
* future intent may not create arrival.

⸻

10. Universal target validation

CanonValidator must reject, pending, or defer proposals with unsafe target binding.

Target risks:

target missing
target ambiguous
target generic when persistence required
target reference-only when presence required
target sanitizer low confidence
target belongs to disabled system
target appears only inside quote/dream/rumor

Unknown targets may be allowed for some event types as candidates, but not for durable canon if exact identity matters.

⸻

11. Duplicate validation

CanonValidator must check duplicate risk before reducers commit repeat-sensitive events.

Repeat-sensitive families:

inventory acquisition
item transfer
item consumption/destruction
reward payment
XP gain
level up
stat gain
time advance
age update
ability stabilization
ability use evidence if durable
quest objective completion
quest completion
card write/create/update
startup import
migration import/quarantine action

If operation key exists:

status = duplicate_skipped
canonAllowed = false or reducerAllowed = false depending case

Duplicate skip must be audited.

⸻

12. Inventory canon rules

Inventory canon requires confirmed acquisition, transfer, consumption, loss, or custody change.

12.1 Accept inventory acquisition only when

All are true:

source is output or validated setup/manual future path
local clause confirms actual acquisition/handoff/receipt/pickup
not negated
not failed
not revoked
not hypothetical
not quoted instruction only
not dream/vision
not abstract/metaphorical
not visible-only
not available-only
item target is bound
operation key not duplicate

12.2 Reject inventory acquisition when

Any are true:

deniedByLocalClause
revokedByLocalClause
failedOrRevoked
abstractOrMetaphorical
quotedSpeech as instruction only
dreamOrVision
visibleOnly
availableOnly
futureIntent without handoff
attemptOnly from input

12.3 Pending inventory cases

Use pending when text suggests possible item access but not ownership.

Examples:

You see a silver ring on the table.
The goblin drops a rusty dagger.
Mara offers you the key.
The chest contains a potion.

These may become scene item, available loot, or offer candidates later, but not owned inventory.

12.4 Mixed clause rule

In:

Mira refuses to give you the jeweled crown, but she hands you the red healing potion.

Validation result:

crown rejected
a potion handoff proposal may be accepted if otherwise safe

12.5 Inventory tests

You decide not to take the rusted iron key -> reject acquisition
You try to grab the key, but the goblin snatches it away -> reject acquisition
You pick up the idea of a brass key -> reject abstract
In a dream, you receive Crown of Dawn -> reject real acquisition
Mara says "Take the key" -> reject acquisition, possible offer/speech only
Mira hands you the red healing potion -> accept handoff candidate if key not duplicate

⸻

13. Ability canon rules

Ability canon must distinguish training evidence, attempt, failed use, output-only claim, candidate, unstable, stable, sealed, and unavailable.

13.1 Accept ability training evidence when

source is input or output
explicit training/practice/refine/drill language exists
ability target is cleanly bound
not pretend-only
not quoted-only
not dream-only
not denied/no usable technique

Input training normally creates or updates candidate/evidence, not stable mastery.

13.2 Accept ability stable creation only when

Strong confirmation exists, such as:

repeated training culmination
teacher confirms learned technique and player demonstrates it
successful controlled manifestation
setup-structured starting ability before startup lock
validated transformation/grant source

13.3 Reject or pending output-only learning claims

Examples:

You learned Moonblade.
You unlocked Star Chain.
New skill: Mirror Guard.

Default status:

pending or rejected unless supported by training/source evidence

13.4 Reject ability canon when

quoted instruction only
pretend use
failed/fizzled/collapsed/no effect
scroll title or rumor only
manual narrative title only
input attempt only with no output consequence

13.5 Ability name rule

CanonValidator must ensure ability names were cleaned by AbilityNameSanitizer, not shared destructive cleaner.

Reject or pending if target appears polluted:

Ember Ward to protect allies from heat
Frost Step by moving across slick stones
Stoneguard until my stance holds under impact

Correct targets:

Ember Ward
Frost Step
Stoneguard

13.6 Ability tests

I train Ember Ward to protect allies from heat -> training evidence/candidate allowed
You learned Moonblade -> no stable ability by default
You attempt Void Lantern Veil, but it collapses -> failure evidence, no stable ability
Mira says "Use Hollow Star Palm," but you do not know it -> no ability
I travel to the smithy -> no smithy ability

⸻

14. Body/status/death canon rules

Body canon requires direct, literal body outcome.

14.1 Accept status gain when

output directly states player status
status target is known
not quoted/dream/rumor/metaphor
severity wording matches status

Examples:

Your legs shake and you are exhausted by the climb.
Your arm bleeds from the wolf bite.
Your palm is burned and blistered.

14.2 Distinguish fatigue and exhaustion

fatigued / weary / worn out -> fatigued
exhausted / can barely stand / drained completely -> exhausted

Do not upgrade fatigue to exhaustion without strong wording.

14.3 Accept recovery when

output directly states bleeding stops, poison fades, breath steadies, burns heal, exhaustion lifts

14.4 Reject death/body changes from

people say you died
Mara says "you are dead"
in a dream you die
you feel dead tired
symbolic death
threats or insults

14.5 Death boundary rule

Changing deathBoundary requires explicit direct outcome and later high-safety validation.

Rumor, quote, dream, threat, metaphor, or nonfatal hostile action cannot set death.

⸻

15. Quest canon rules

Quest canon must distinguish rumor, offer, acceptance, active objective, objective progress, objective completion, ready turn-in, completed, rewarded, failed, and abandoned.

15.1 Accept quest acceptance when

player explicitly accepts in input and output/structure supports quest start
or output clearly says quest accepted/started
or setup structured quest starts active before lock

15.2 Pending quest when

NPC asks/offers/request but player has not accepted
rumor of job exists
quest board lists possible task

15.3 Objective breadth

CanonValidator must allow objective families:

recover
find
repair
escort
clear
deliver
investigate
protect
bring
retrieve
rescue
defeat
survey

But objective text is not automatically completed.

15.4 Reject quest canon from

rumor only
quoted hypothetical request
future promise only
quest mentioned in card/context/command output

⸻

16. Reward canon rules

Reward canon must separate promise, ready, payment, disputed, denied, and paid.

16.1 Promise is not payment

Old Bren promises to pay you 15 gold if you clear the wolves.

Validation:

reward.promise accepted or pending as quest reward promise
currency payment rejected

16.2 Payment accepted only when

output directly confirms payment/handoff
reward target/amount is bound
payer/source is plausible
not denied/disputed/withheld
operation reward key not duplicate

16.3 Reject payment when

promises to pay
will pay later
could pay
refuses to pay
denies payment
mentions reward amount only
command /quests displays reward
Story Card says reward paid

16.4 Reward duplicate rule

If reward key exists:

duplicate_skipped
no currency added

⸻

17. Place/scene canon rules

Place canon must distinguish current scene confirmation, strong arrival, weak mention, visible/nearby reference, future intent, travel intent, and route/interstitial.

17.1 Current scene confirmation accepted when

output says current location remains/still/around you/you stand in current place
place target matches or confirms existing current scene

17.2 Arrival accepted when

output uses strong entry/arrival wording
named destination target is bound
not hypothetical/future/quoted/dream

Strong arrival verbs:

enter
step into
walk inside
arrive at
return to
descend into
push through doors into

17.3 Weak mention rejected as scene overwrite

Reject current scene overwrite from:

visible from
nearby
down the street
beyond
road to
only consider going
mentions/talks about
future plan

17.4 Route/interstitial rule

In:

After the road, you enter The Drunken Huntsman.

The destination is:

The Drunken Huntsman

not:

road

17.5 Input travel intent

Input like:

I head toward the chapel.

may create travel intent, not arrival.

⸻

18. Time/aging canon rules

Time canon requires direct literal time passage.

18.1 Accept time advance when

output literally states time passes
not quoted
not dream
not pretend/story practice
not negated by no time passes
operation key not duplicate

Examples:

A week passes during the journey.
Twenty years pass in exile.
After two years, the city rebuilds.

18.2 Reject time/age mutation from

"Twenty years pass," you say
in a dream twenty years pass
practice narration
no time passes
wake as same age
hypothetical future time

18.3 Aging rule

Age update is separate from time text update.

A long time passage may require body/species/age policy before changing age.

If age rules are unknown, mark pending rather than inventing.

18.4 Duplicate time rule

Same literal time passage output cannot advance time/age twice.

⸻

19. Actor canon rules

Actor canon must distinguish present actor, scene-only actor, persistent actor, absent actor, reference-only actor, rumor/claim, and memory.

19.1 Accept present actor when

output directly places actor in current scene
actor target bound
not negated/absent/reference-only

19.2 Reject present actor when

actor is definitely not here
actor is only mentioned
actor appears in dream/vision
training dummy named after actor
actor is elsewhere
actor is quoted/referenced in speech only

19.3 Compound actor rule

If resolver suppressed generic shorter actor, CanonValidator must preserve that decision.

Veteran Guard -> one actor target, not Guard + Veteran Guard

19.4 Witness memory rule

Witness memory may be accepted only for actors present or directly receiving a report.

Absent actors do not receive scene memory.

Private reports do not become public reputation unless reputation system validates that later.

⸻

20. Progression canon rules

Progression is conservative in MVP.

20.1 XP accepted only when

output explicitly grants XP
amount is bound
source is valid
operation key not duplicate
progression feature stage allows XP commit

20.2 Reject XP from vague wording

This was a learning experience.
You gain experience in life.
You feel wiser.

These are not numeric XP.

20.3 Level/stat gains

Level/stat changes require explicit validated trigger and formula support.

If formula automation is not active, mark pending/deferred rather than mutating.

⸻

21. Card/migration canon rules

Card and migration validation must not route to gameplay reducers.

21.1 Card validation

Accepted card proposals may route only to CardSyncLite mirror behavior.

They cannot create gameplay canon.

Generated cards remain deferred.

Manual card text is not canon.

21.2 Migration validation

Migration may validate structural repair or quarantine.

It may not validate old card prose into inventory, ability, quest, or actor canon.

Unsafe old data becomes quarantine or ignored.

⸻

22. Deferred system rules

If a proposal belongs to a disabled system, CanonValidator returns deferred or blocked.

Deferred systems:

economy
shop/buy/sell/barter/debt
crafting/materials/recipes
investigation/cases/evidence
reputation/factions/titles automation
domain/labor/workers/projects
absorption/evolution/devour
advanced combat/warfare/scale/group combat
party/summons advanced controls
multi-protagonist
generated CardSync beta
manual mutation commands

Deferred means:

no gameplay mutation
honest audit/releasecheck note
future mini-spec required

⸻

23. CanonValidator audit format

Validation audits must explain decisions compactly.

Examples:

Accepted inventory.handoff_candidate red_healing_potion: direct handoff confirmed, no blocking flags.
Rejected inventory.acquire_candidate rusted_iron_key: local clause denied acquisition.
Rejected ability.output_only_learning_claim moonblade: output-only unsupported learning claim.
Pending quest.offer_candidate repair_north_wall: offered/requested but not accepted.
Duplicate skipped reward.payment_candidate 15_gold: reward key already exists.

Do not dump full raw text.

⸻

24. Validation caps

Recommended caps:

maxValidationResultsPerTurn = 25
maxValidationAuditLinesPerTurn = 12
maxRejectionReasonsPerProposal = 5
maxAuditPreviewLength = 180

If capped:

* preserve accepted/duplicate/failures first
* summarize omitted low-risk pending/rejected items
* do not crash

⸻

25. Reducer handoff

CanonValidator output to ReducerRegistry must include only accepted reducer-allowed results.

Handoff:

AcceptedValidationBatch {
  accepted: ValidationResult[],
  rejected: ValidationResult[],
  pending: ValidationResult[],
  deferred: ValidationResult[],
  duplicateSkipped: ValidationResult[],
  malformed: ValidationResult[],
  auditSummary: string[]
}

ReducerRegistry may only process:

accepted where reducerAllowed = true

Everything else is audit/evidence only.

⸻

26. Required CanonValidator tests

BC11-001 — Command output blocked

Proposal source from [AIDRPG] Inventory.

Expected:

status = blocked
canonAllowed = false
reducerAllowed = false

⸻

BC11-002 — Context text blocked

Proposal source context.

Expected:

blocked
no reducer

⸻

BC11-003 — Story Card text blocked

Proposal source story card.

Expected:

blocked
no gameplay canon

⸻

BC11-004 — Input item attempt not acquisition

Input:

I take the jeweled crown.

Expected:

pending or intent-only
not accepted owned inventory

⸻

BC11-005 — Denied item rejected

Output:

You decide not to take the rusted iron key.

Expected:

rejected inventory acquisition

⸻

BC11-006 — Failed grab rejected

Output:

You try to grab the rusted iron key, but the goblin snatches it away first.

Expected:

rejected acquisition

⸻

BC11-007 — Mixed clause inventory

Output:

Mira refuses to give you the jeweled crown, but she hands you the red healing potion.

Expected:

crown rejected
potion accepted if otherwise safe

⸻

BC11-008 — Abstract item rejected

Output:

You pick up the idea of a brass key.

Expected:

rejected abstract inventory

⸻

BC11-009 — Dream item rejected

Output:

In a dream, you receive Crown of Dawn, then wake empty-handed.

Expected:

rejected real acquisition

⸻

BC11-010 — Ability training allowed as evidence

Input:

I train Ember Ward to protect allies from heat.

Expected:

accepted or pending as training evidence/candidate
not stable mastery

⸻

BC11-011 — Output-only learned ability not stable

Output:

You learned Moonblade.

Expected:

rejected or pending unsupported learning claim
no stable ability reducer allowed

⸻

BC11-012 — Failed ability rejected as stable

Output:

You attempt Void Lantern Veil, but the veil collapses and no usable technique remains.

Expected:

failure evidence maybe
stable ability rejected

⸻

BC11-013 — Exhausted accepted distinctly

Output:

Your legs shake and you are exhausted by the climb.

Expected:

accepted body status exhausted

⸻

BC11-014 — Fatigue does not auto-exhaust

Output:

You are fatigued and worn out.

Expected:

fatigued accepted
exhausted rejected/not created

⸻

BC11-015 — Death rumor rejected

Output:

People say you died in the ruins.

Expected:

death boundary rejected
rumor/claim maybe recorded later

⸻

BC11-016 — Weak scene reference rejected as overwrite

Output:

The chapel is visible from the market lane.

Expected:

no chapel current-scene acceptance

⸻

BC11-017 — Strong arrival accepted

Output:

You enter the chapel.

Expected:

place arrival accepted

⸻

BC11-018 — Named arrival beats road

Output:

After the road, you enter The Drunken Huntsman.

Expected:

arrival target The Drunken Huntsman
not road

⸻

BC11-019 — Nonliteral time rejected

Output:

You say, "Twenty years pass," while practicing narration. No time passes.

Expected:

time/age mutation rejected

⸻

BC11-020 — Literal time pending/accepted

Output:

Twenty years pass in exile.

Expected:

time advance accepted or pending depending age policy
not duplicate

⸻

BC11-021 — Reward promise not payment

Output:

Old Bren promises to pay you 15 gold if you clear the wolves.

Expected:

reward promise accepted/pending
payment rejected

⸻

BC11-022 — Reward duplicate skipped

Reward payment proposal with existing reward key.

Expected:

duplicate_skipped
no reducer allowed

⸻

BC11-023 — Absent actor not present

Output:

The goblin is definitely not here.

Expected:

present actor rejected
absent/reference-only possible

⸻

BC11-024 — Compound actor preserved

Output:

A veteran guard stands nearby.

Expected:

Veteran Guard accepted/pending as one actor
Guard duplicate rejected or never proposed

⸻

BC11-025 — XP vague wording rejected

Output:

This was a learning experience.

Expected:

no XP gain accepted

⸻

BC11-026 — Deferred crafting blocked

Proposal family crafting.

Expected:

deferred or blocked
no reducer

⸻

BC11-027 — Validation audit compact

Expected:

audit line explains decision
no full giant raw text dump

⸻

27. ReleaseCheck requirements for Build Chunk 11

ReleaseCheck must eventually verify:

CanonValidator exists
ValidationResult schema exists
unsafe sources blocked
input intent cannot become completed success
local clause flags used
family-specific validators exist or are honestly shell-only
inventory negative/dream/quote/abstract rejection exists
ability output-only/failed/quoted rejection exists
body/death rumor/quote rejection exists
scene weak/strong distinction exists
time nonliteral guard exists
reward promise/payment separation exists
actor absent/reference-only guard exists
duplicate-sensitive validation checks ledger
reducers cannot receive non-accepted results
validation audit exists
validation caps exist

ReleaseCheck must fail if:

ProposalBuilder output can reach reducers without CanonValidator
CanonValidator mutates gameplay state
command/context/card/audit text can become canon
quoted/dreamed/rumored/revoked/denied text can directly mutate relevant gameplay state
promised reward can pay currency
input-only item/ability/travel can become completed success

⸻

29. What Build Chunk 12 should do next

Build Chunk 12 — Resolver and Owner-Specific Sanitizer Plan

Build Chunk 12 must define:

* shared conservative cleaner
